# Results outside the zero-adjoined-ring series

This file records independent analytic, representation-theoretic, or combinatorial results whose statements do not depend on the zero-adjoined ring. Source occurrence tags are locators, not a check certificate. Intake remains in progress.

## paper1_remaining_zeta_other_results

# Remaining zeta-workbench prose claims

This is a bounded source-intake list, not a proof audit. The statements below paraphrase the source claims and are outside the zero-adjoined-ring series because their ordinary analytic, differential, matrix, or finite-dimensional algebra assertions do not require zero adjunction. Their truth, numerical certificates, and imported fluid-witness hypotheses have not been independently checked in this pass. Formal statements already assigned OTHER-WB-001–092 retain those identifiers in the crosswalk. Source prose is not reproduced.

Inspection covered all subsection headings, selected opening passages, and the explicitly identified supported-zero passages in the 34 exact files listed in the coverage table. It does not claim an exhaustive extraction of every equation or a complete reading of every proof. Subsection ranges identify the source location of each grouped claim.

## paper3_other_results

# Results outside Paper 3's zero-adjoined-ring scope

The following claims occur in corpus03–04 or satellite19 but do not intrinsically use R0. They are not imported as theorems of this series merely because a support analogy was proposed.

- Cyclic Fourier augmentation is the trivial character coordinate (corpus03, corpus04; satellite19 Fourier proposition).
- The cyclic augmentation lattice is A_(r−1), independently of zero adjunction; only its explicitly chosen support linearization is discussed in Paper3.
- A3 and D3 are isometric root lattices; SU4/Spin6 comparison is independent Lie theory.
- The Eisenstein A2 norm and Epstein factorization 6·2^(−s)ζ(s)L(s,χ−3) are independent analytic number theory.
- Endpoint principal-part bases, shifted completed-Hurwitz residues, invariant/anti-invariant decomposition and logarithmic Jacobian are analytic identities, allocated to the analytic audit rather than Paper3.
- The endpoint Boolean comultiplication and λ−λ² coefficient defect belong to the coefficient/Dirichlet-series calculation; they do not construct a C*-extension.
- The coprime double Dirichlet kernel and the regularized value −3 are separate analytic continuations.
- The S3 orbifold label count and quantum-dimension vector are finite-group representation calculations.
- The finite Weyl operator basis and twisted group algebra M_r(C) are independent matrix algebra.
- The CUE Weyl product, first derivative moment sum of squares, and block vector-space isomorphism with sl_(s+1) are independent random-matrix/linear-algebra claims; a vector-space isomorphism alone is not a Lie functor.
- The N=3 quadratic sum-of-squares obstruction is a real-polynomial statement independent of R0.
- Shioda–Tate ranks and elliptic-fibration root configurations are external algebraic-geometric theorems, not consequences of an augmentation row.
- The 12A2/Niemeier/Leech/moonshine and F-theory comparisons have no defined functor from zero-adjoined semimodules in these sources.
- A displayed first-order differential expression without a specified closed operator domain does not determine an APS index (satellite19a domain audit); no index from that expression enters Paper3.
- The artificial symmetric C2 enrichment in satellite19d is an additional direct-sum extension construction, not an intrinsic feature of R0; the paper instead states exactly where real character classes coincide.


## paper3_packet_other_results

# Packet and phase results outside the split-zero papers

This is a statement-level intake inventory, not a claim that every excluded analytic proof has been audited. The retained constructions were independently derived before the specified original proofs were consulted; see the separate check note. Every formal source statement has a crosswalk row. Repeated statements share an identifier. No novelty or human check is claimed.

- **SZ2-PKT-015** — The ratio of shifted Dedekind zeta functions has multiplicative ideal coefficients obtained by expanding each local geometric factor.
